Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the trees framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 619. The resulting operator achieves bound O(762/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_619(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 619. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^762(S, F) is trivial. QED.
